# dashboard-planning
# ============================================================
# PT BIMA | PROCUREMENT DASHBOARD
# ============================================================
library(shiny)
library(bslib)
library(plotly)
library(DT)
library(dplyr)
library(readxl)
library(lubridate)
library(openxlsx)
library(rsconnect)

DATA_FILE <- "C:/Users/Gunaran/Downloads/Dashboard Perencanaan/Dashboard/Dataset mentah dashboard.xlsx"
DATA_SHEET <- "Sheet1"
ALL <- "__ALL__"

# ------------------------- DATA ------------------------------
if (!file.exists(DATA_FILE)) {
  stop("File Excel tidak ditemukan. Sesuaikan DATA_FILE di bagian atas script.")
}
raw_data <- read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet = DATA_SHEET)
required_columns <- c("Document Date", "Purchasing Document", "Site", "Area",
                      "Name of Supplier", "Material", "Mat Group Desc",
                      "Short Text", "Order Quantity", "Net Order Value", "Merk")
missing_columns <- setdiff(required_columns, names(raw_data))
if (length(missing_columns)) {
  stop(paste("Kolom Excel belum lengkap:", paste(missing_columns, collapse = ", ")))
}
as_po_date <- function(x) {
  if (inherits(x, "Date")) return(x)
  if (inherits(x, "POSIXt")) return(as.Date(x))
  if (is.numeric(x)) return(as.Date(x, origin = "1899-12-30"))
  as.Date(parse_date_time(as.character(x), orders = c("ymd", "dmy", "mdy"), quiet = TRUE))
}
clean_label <- function(x) {
  x <- trimws(as.character(x))
  x[is.na(x) | x == ""] <- "Tidak tercatat"
  x
}
po_data <- raw_data %>%
  transmute(
    document_date = as_po_date(`Document Date`),
    po_number = trimws(as.character(`Purchasing Document`)),
    area = clean_label(Area), site = clean_label(Site),
    supplier = clean_label(`Name of Supplier`),
    material = clean_label(Material),
    material_group = clean_label(`Mat Group Desc`),
    short_text = clean_label(`Short Text`),
    quantity = as.numeric(`Order Quantity`),
    value = as.numeric(`Net Order Value`), brand = clean_label(Merk)
  ) %>%
  filter(!is.na(po_number), po_number != "") %>%
  mutate(year = year(document_date))
if (!nrow(po_data)) stop("Tidak ada baris PO yang dapat ditampilkan.")

# ---------------------- FORMAT ANGKA -------------------------
fmt_num <- function(x, digits = 0) {
  formatC(x, format = "f", digits = digits, big.mark = ".", decimal.mark = ",")
}
fmt_rp <- function(x) paste0("Rp ", fmt_num(x, 2))
money_unit <- function(x) {
  largest <- max(c(0, abs(x)), na.rm = TRUE)
  if (largest >= 1e12) return(list(divisor = 1e12, label = "triliun"))
  if (largest >= 1e9) return(list(divisor = 1e9, label = "miliar"))
  if (largest >= 1e6) return(list(divisor = 1e6, label = "juta"))
  if (largest >= 1e3) return(list(divisor = 1e3, label = "ribu"))
  list(divisor = 1, label = "")
}
compact_rp <- function(x) {
  unit <- money_unit(x)
  trimws(paste("Rp", fmt_num(x / unit$divisor, 2), unit$label))
}
fmt_date <- function(x) ifelse(is.na(x), "—", format(x, "%d/%m/%Y"))
escape_text <- function(x) as.character(htmltools::htmlEscape(as.character(x)))
wrap_label <- function(x, width = 30) {
  vapply(x, function(label) paste(escape_text(strwrap(label, width)), collapse = "<br>"),
    character(1))
}
latest_date <- function(x) {
  if (all(is.na(x))) as.Date(NA) else max(x, na.rm = TRUE)
}
BLUE <- "#185ADB"
PALETTE <- c("#185ADB", "#22A699", "#F2B84B", "#F28C62", "#8C73D9", "#A9B7CA")
plot_config <- function(p) {
  config(p, displayModeBar = FALSE, responsive = TRUE, scrollZoom = FALSE)
}
check_rows <- function(data) {
  validate(need(nrow(data) > 0, "Tidak ada data untuk kombinasi filter ini."))
}

# --------------------- FILTER PER MENU -----------------------
filter_ui <- function(id) {
  ns <- NS(id)
  sidebar(width = 260,
    div(class = "filter-title", "Filter data"),
    p(class = "muted", "Atur cakupan analisis pada menu ini."),
    selectInput(ns("year"), "Tahun", choices = c("Semua tahun" = ALL,
      setNames(as.character(sort(unique(na.omit(po_data$year)), decreasing = TRUE)),
               as.character(sort(unique(na.omit(po_data$year)), decreasing = TRUE))))),
    selectInput(ns("area"), "Area", choices = c("Semua area" = ALL,
      setNames(sort(unique(po_data$area)), sort(unique(po_data$area))))),
    selectizeInput(ns("site"), "Site", choices = c("Semua site" = ALL,
      setNames(sort(unique(po_data$site)), sort(unique(po_data$site))))),
    actionButton(ns("reset"), "Reset filter", class = "btn-outline-secondary w-100"),
    hr(), p(class = "filter-note",
      "Nilai pengadaan berasal dari Net Order Value. Jumlah PO dihitung berdasarkan nomor PO unik.")
  )
}
filter_server <- function(id) {
  moduleServer(id, function(input, output, session) {
    observeEvent(input$area, {
      choices <- po_data
      if (!is.null(input$area) && input$area != ALL) choices <- filter(choices, area == input$area)
      sites <- sort(unique(choices$site))
      current <- isolate(input$site)
      selected <- if (!is.null(current) && current %in% c(ALL, sites)) current else ALL
      updateSelectizeInput(session, "site", choices = c("Semua site" = ALL, setNames(sites,
        sites)), selected = selected)
    }, ignoreNULL = TRUE)
    observeEvent(input$reset, {
      updateSelectInput(session, "year", selected = ALL)
      updateSelectInput(session, "area", selected = ALL)
      updateSelectizeInput(session, "site", selected = ALL)
    })
    area_site_data <- reactive({
      req(input$area, input$site)
      data <- po_data
      if (input$area != ALL) data <- filter(data, area == input$area)
      if (input$site != ALL) data <- filter(data, site == input$site)
      data
    })
    data <- reactive({
      req(input$year)
      result <- area_site_data()
      if (input$year != ALL) result <- filter(result, year == as.integer(input$year))
      result
    })
    list(data = data, base = area_site_data, year = reactive(input$year))
  })
}

# ---------------------- GRAFIK BATANG -------------------------
rank_chart <- function(data, label, metric, money = FALSE, axis_label = "Jumlah PO unik") {
  check_rows(data)
  data <- data[order(-data[[metric]], as.character(data[[label]])), , drop = FALSE]
  data <- head(data, 10)
  labels <- as.character(data[[label]])
  values <- data[[metric]]
  unit <- if (money) money_unit(values) else list(divisor = 1, label = "")
  plotted <- values / unit$divisor
  shown <- if (money) fmt_num(plotted, 2) else fmt_num(values)
  ids <- paste0("row_", seq_along(labels))
  tips <- paste0(escape_text(labels), "<br>",
    if (money) fmt_rp(values) else paste(fmt_num(values), axis_label))
  tick_label <- paste0(seq_along(labels), ". ", wrap_label(labels))
  span <- max(c(abs(plotted), 1), na.rm = TRUE)
  plot_ly(x = plotted, y = ids, type = "bar", orientation = "h", height = 560,
    text = shown, textposition = "outside", cliponaxis = FALSE,
    hovertext = tips, hovertemplate = "%{hovertext}<extra></extra>",
    marker = list(color = c("#1644A8", rep("#538DE8", max(0, length(ids) - 1)))),
    textfont = list(size = 12, color = "#24344D")) %>%
    layout(showlegend = FALSE, bargap = 0.35, paper_bgcolor = "white", plot_bgcolor = "white",
      font = list(family = "Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif", size = 12, color = "#52627A"),
      margin = list(l = 240, r = 90, t = 25, b = 65), separators = ",.",
      xaxis = list(title = if (money) trimws(paste("Nilai PO (Rp", unit$label,
        ")")) else axis_label,
        range = c(min(c(0, plotted)) - if (any(plotted < 0)) span * 0.15 else 0,
                  max(c(0, plotted)) + span * 0.23),
        tickformat = if (money) ",.2f" else ",d", nticks = 6,
        gridcolor = "#EDF1F7", zeroline = FALSE, automargin = TRUE),
      yaxis = list(title = "", type = "category", categoryorder = "array", categoryarray = rev(ids),
        tickmode = "array", tickvals = ids, ticktext = tick_label,
        showgrid = FALSE, automargin = TRUE)) %>% plot_config()
}

# ---------------------- GRAFIK DONAT --------------------------
donut_chart <- function(data, label, metric = "total_value") {
  check_rows(data)
  validate(need(all(data[[metric]] >= 0),
    "Distribusi donat tidak tersedia untuk kategori bernilai negatif. Lihat grafik batang atau tabel detail."))
  data <- data[data[[metric]] > 0, , drop = FALSE]
  check_rows(data)
  data <- data[order(-data[[metric]], as.character(data[[label]])), , drop = FALSE]
  total <- sum(data[[metric]])
  part <- head(data, 5)
  labels <- as.character(part[[label]])
  values <- part[[metric]]
  if (nrow(data) > 5) {
    labels <- c(labels, "Lainnya (di luar 5 besar)")
    values <- c(values, sum(data[[metric]][-seq_len(5)]))
  }
  share <- 100 * values / total
  legend <- paste0(wrap_label(labels, 26), " · ", fmt_num(share, 1), "%")
  tips <- paste0(escape_text(labels), "<br>", fmt_rp(values), "<br>", fmt_num(share, 1),
    "% dari total nilai PO")
  plot_ly(labels = legend, values = values, type = "pie", hole = 0.69,
    sort = FALSE, direction = "clockwise", textinfo = "none", height = 560,
    hovertext = tips, hovertemplate = "%{hovertext}<extra></extra>",
    marker = list(colors = PALETTE, line = list(color = "white", width = 3)),
    domain = list(x = c(0.06, 0.94), y = c(0.28, 0.98))) %>%
    layout(showlegend = TRUE, paper_bgcolor = "white", plot_bgcolor = "white",
      margin = list(l = 20, r = 20, t = 20, b = 10),
      font = list(family = "Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif", color = "#52627A", size = 12),
      legend = list(orientation = "h", x = 0.5, xanchor = "center", y = 0.16, yanchor = "top"),
      annotations = list(list(x = 0.5, y = 0.63, xref = "paper", yref = "paper", showarrow = FALSE,
        text = paste0("<b>", compact_rp(total), "</b><br>Total nilai PO"),
        font = list(size = 17, color = "#17345D")))) %>% plot_config()
}

# --------------------- TABEL PROFESIONAL ----------------------
make_table <- function(data, money = character(), counts = character(), decimals = character(),
  page_length = 10) {
  # Date tetap ISO untuk pengurutan kronologis; render tampilan dd/mm/yyyy.
  date_columns <- names(data)[vapply(data, inherits, logical(1), what = "Date")]
  for (column in date_columns) data[[column]] <- as.character(data[[column]])
  defs <- list()
  if (length(date_columns)) defs <- list(list(
    targets = match(date_columns, names(data)) - 1L,
    render = JS("function(data,type,row,meta){if(!data)return type==='display'?'—':''; if(type==='display'||type==='filter'){var p=data.split('-');return p[2]+'/'+p[1]+'/'+p[0];}return data;}")))
  table <- datatable(data, rownames = FALSE, escape = TRUE,
    class = "stripe hover compact", selection = "none",
    options = list(pageLength = page_length, lengthMenu = c(10, 20, 50), scrollX = TRUE,
      autoWidth = TRUE, order = list(), columnDefs = defs,
      language = list(search = "Cari:", lengthMenu = "Tampilkan _MENU_ baris",
        info = "_START_–_END_ dari _TOTAL_ baris", infoEmpty = "Tidak ada data",
        zeroRecords = "Tidak ada data yang sesuai", emptyTable = "Tidak ada data untuk filter ini",
        paginate = list(previous = "Sebelumnya", "next" = "Berikutnya"))))
  if (length(money)) table <- formatCurrency(table, money, currency = "Rp ", digits = 2,
    interval = 3, mark = ".", dec.mark = ",")
  if (length(counts)) table <- formatRound(table, counts, digits = 0,
    interval = 3, mark = ".", dec.mark = ",")
  if (length(decimals)) table <- formatRound(table, decimals, digits = 2,
    interval = 3, mark = ".", dec.mark = ",")
  numeric_columns <- c(money, counts, decimals)
  if (length(numeric_columns)) table <- formatStyle(table, numeric_columns,
    textAlign = "right", fontVariantNumeric = "tabular-nums", whiteSpace = "nowrap")
  table
}

# -------------------- KOMPONEN TAMPILAN -----------------------
page_title <- function(eyebrow, title, description) {
  div(class = "page-heading", div(class = "eyebrow", eyebrow), h2(title), p(description))
}
chart_card <- function(title, subtitle, id) {
  card(class = "chart-card", full_screen = TRUE, min_height = "655px",
    card_header(div(class = "chart-title", title), div(class = "chart-subtitle", subtitle)),
    plotlyOutput(id, height = "560px"))
}
kpi_card <- function(title, id) {
  card(class = "kpi-card", div(class = "kpi-label", title),
    div(class = "kpi-value", textOutput(id)), uiOutput(paste0(id, "_yoy")))
}
insight_box <- function(id) uiOutput(id)
analysis_tab <- function(label, prefix, category) {
  nav_panel(label,
    div(class = "analysis-content",
      insight_box(paste0(prefix, "_insight")),
      chart_card(paste("10", category, "dengan Nilai PO Terbesar"),
        "Peringkat berdasarkan jumlah Net Order Value; nilai lengkap tersedia pada tooltip.",
          paste0(prefix, "_value")),
      chart_card(paste("Distribusi Nilai PO per", category),
        "Lima kategori terbesar dan sisanya sebagai Lainnya; persentase berdasarkan nilai pengadaan.", paste0(prefix, "_distribution")),
      chart_card(paste("10", category, "dengan PO Terbanyak"),
        "Frekuensi dihitung dari nomor PO unik pada setiap kategori.", paste0(prefix,
          "_frequency")),
      card(card_header(paste("Detail", category)), DTOutput(paste0(prefix, "_table"))))
  )
}

ui <- page_navbar(
  title = div(
    class = "corporate-header",
    div(
      class = "brand-title",
      strong("PT BIMA"),
      span("PROCUREMENT ANALYTICS")
    ),
    div(
      class = "corporate-logos",
      tags$img(
        src = logo_danantara,
        alt = "Danantara Indonesia",
        class = "logo-danantara"
      ),
      tags$img(
        src = logo_pelindo,
        alt = "Pelindo",
        class = "logo-pelindo"
      ),
      div(
        class = "logo-bima-frame",
        tags$img(
          src = logo_bima,
          alt = "PT BIMA",
          class = "logo-bima"
        )
      )
    )
  ),
  id = "main_nav", fillable = FALSE,
  theme = bs_theme(version = 5, primary = BLUE, bg = "#F3F6FB", fg = "#24344D",
    base_font = "Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif"),
  header = tags$head(tags$style(HTML("
    body {font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;background:#F3F6FB;}
    .navbar {
      background:#fff!important;
      border-bottom:1px solid #E3EAF4;
      padding:18px 28px 12px;
    }
    .navbar > .container-fluid,
    .navbar > .container {
      flex-wrap:wrap!important;
      column-gap:16px;
      row-gap:16px;
    }
    .navbar > .container-fluid > .navbar-brand,
    .navbar > .container > .navbar-brand {
      flex:1 1 calc(100% - 80px);
      min-width:0;
      margin:0;
      padding:0;
      white-space:normal;
    }
    .corporate-header {
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      justify-content:space-between;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
      gap:18px 32px;
      width:100%;
      min-height:76px;
    }
    .corporate-header > .brand-title {
      border-right:1px solid #DDE5F0;
      padding-right:32px;
      flex-shrink:0;
      gap:5px;
    }
    .corporate-logos {
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      justify-content:flex-end;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
      gap:24px;
      margin-left:auto;
    }
    .logo-danantara,
    .logo-pelindo {
      display:block;
      width:175px;
      height:56px;
      object-fit:contain;
      flex-shrink:0;
    }
    .logo-bima-frame {
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      justify-content:center;
      width:170px;
      height:64px;
      overflow:hidden;
      flex-shrink:0;
    }
    .logo-bima {
      display:block;
      width:170px;
      height:170px;
      max-width:none;
      object-fit:contain;
      flex-shrink:0;
    }
    .navbar .navbar-collapse {
      flex-basis:100%!important;
      width:100%;
      padding-top:14px;
      border-top:1px solid #EDF1F7;
    }
    .navbar .navbar-nav {align-items:center;gap:12px;}
    .navbar .navbar-toggler {flex-shrink:0;}
    @media(max-width:991.98px) {
      .navbar {padding:14px 18px;}
      .corporate-header {gap:16px 24px;}
      .corporate-logos {gap:18px;}
      .logo-danantara,.logo-pelindo {width:140px;height:46px;}
      .logo-bima-frame {width:140px;height:54px;}
      .logo-bima {width:140px;height:140px;}
      .navbar .navbar-nav {align-items:stretch;gap:8px;}
    }
    @media(max-width:650px) {
      .navbar > .container-fluid > .navbar-brand,
      .navbar > .container > .navbar-brand {flex-basis:calc(100% - 64px);}
      .corporate-header > .brand-title {border:0;padding-right:0;}
      .corporate-header > .brand-title strong {font-size:20px;}
      .corporate-header > .brand-title span {font-size:9px;letter-spacing:1px;}
      .corporate-logos {justify-content:flex-start;margin-left:0;gap:12px;}
      .logo-danantara,.logo-pelindo {width:115px;height:40px;}
      .logo-bima-frame {width:115px;height:44px;}
      .logo-bima {width:115px;height:115px;}
    }
    .brand-title {display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1.35;}
    .brand-title strong {color:#14366B;font-size:22px;letter-spacing:1px;}
    .brand-title span {color:#7B8BA2;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1.5px;}
    .navbar .nav-link {font-weight:600;font-size:14px;padding:12px 20px!important;border-radius:9px;line-height:1.5;}
    .navbar .nav-link.active {color:#185ADB!important;background:#EDF3FF;}
    .bslib-sidebar-layout {border:none;border-radius:0;background:transparent;}
    .bslib-sidebar-layout>.sidebar {background:#fff;border-right:1px solid #E3EAF4;}
    .bslib-sidebar-layout>.main {padding:26px;overflow-x:hidden;min-width:0;}
    .filter-title {font-size:17px;font-weight:700;color:#17345D;margin-top:10px;}
    .muted,.filter-note {font-size:12px;color:#7B8BA2;line-height:1.7;}
    .control-label {font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin-top:10px;}
    .form-control,.selectize-input {border-radius:8px;border-color:#DDE5F0;}
    .page-heading {margin-bottom:24px;}
    .eyebrow {font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1.8px;color:#7290BA;margin-bottom:8px;}
    h2 {font-size:26px;font-weight:700;color:#17345D;margin-bottom:8px;}
    .page-heading p {font-size:13px;color:#75859B;margin:0;line-height:1.7;}
    .card {border:1px solid #E5EBF4;border-radius:14px;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(31,55,92,.035);margin-bottom:22px;}
    .card-header {background:#fff;border-bottom:1px solid #EDF1F7;padding:18px 22px;font-weight:600;}
    .card-body {padding:20px;min-width:0;}
    .chart-title {font-size:16px;font-weight:700;color:#24344D;}
    .chart-subtitle {font-size:12px;font-weight:400;color:#7B8BA2;margin-top:5px;line-height:1.6;}
    .chart-card {flex-shrink:0;}
    .kpi-grid {display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:16px;}
    .kpi-card {padding:20px;margin-bottom:22px;gap:12px;}
    .kpi-label {font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:#75859B;}
    .kpi-value {font-size:clamp(20px,1.8vw,29px);font-weight:750;color:#17345D;line-height:1.45;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}
    .kpi-value,.yoy,.dataTables_wrapper {font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;}
    .yoy {font-size:12px;line-height:1.6;}
    .yoy-up {color:#13866C;}.yoy-down {color:#C84C58;}.yoy-flat {color:#72819A;}
    .yoy-note {font-size:10px;color:#8A97AA;margin-top:4px;line-height:1.5;}
    .insight {background:#EDF4FF;border:1px solid #DCE8FC;border-left:4px solid #185ADB;border-radius:10px;padding:16px 20px;margin-bottom:22px;line-height:1.8;font-size:13px;color:#35527C;}
    .insight strong {color:#17345D;}.insight-title {font-size:10px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1.2px;margin-bottom:5px;color:#185ADB;}
    .analysis-content {padding-top:22px;}.download-actions {display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
    table.dataTable thead th {background:#F6F8FC;color:#52627A;font-size:12px;padding:14px 12px!important;white-space:nowrap;}
    table.dataTable tbody td {font-size:12px;padding:12px!important;vertical-align:middle;}
    .dataTables_wrapper {font-size:12px;color:#667890;}
    @media(max-width:1200px){.kpi-grid {grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}}
    @media(max-width:650px){.kpi-grid {grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:0;}.bslib-sidebar-layout>.main{padding:16px;}h2{font-size:22px;}}
  "))),
  nav_panel("Overview",
    layout_sidebar(sidebar = filter_ui("overview"),
      div(page_title("EXECUTIVE SUMMARY", "Procurement Overview",
        "Ringkasan nilai pengadaan, aktivitas PO, vendor, dan kategori material."),
        div(class = "kpi-grid",
          kpi_card("Total nilai PO", "total_value"), kpi_card("Jumlah PO unik", "total_po"),
          kpi_card("Vendor aktif", "total_vendor"), kpi_card("Kategori material",
            "total_category")),
        insight_box("overview_insight"),
        chart_card("Tren Nilai Pengadaan Bulanan",
          "Nilai berdasarkan bulan Document Date. Bulan tanpa transaksi ditampilkan sebagai nol.",
          "trend"),
        chart_card("10 Vendor dengan Nilai PO Terbesar",
          "Perbandingan kontribusi nilai pengadaan berdasarkan vendor.", "overview_vendor")))) ,
  nav_panel("Vendor",
    layout_sidebar(sidebar = filter_ui("vendor"),
      div(page_title("VENDOR ANALYTICS", "Analisis Vendor",
        "Bandingkan nilai pengadaan, aktivitas PO, dan tanggal PO terakhir setiap vendor."),
        insight_box("vendor_insight"),
        chart_card("10 Vendor dengan Nilai PO Terbesar",
          "Nilai pengadaan dijumlahkan untuk setiap vendor.", "vendor_value"),
        chart_card("10 Vendor dengan PO Terbanyak",
          "Menggunakan jumlah nomor PO unik; satu PO dapat memiliki beberapa baris material.",
          "vendor_frequency"),
        card(card_header("Ringkasan Transaksi Vendor"), DTOutput("vendor_table"))))) ,
  nav_panel("Material & Merek",
    layout_sidebar(sidebar = filter_ui("material"),
      div(page_title("MATERIAL & BRAND ANALYTICS", "Analisis Material & Merek",
        "Analisis terpisah dengan filter Tahun, Area, dan Site yang sama pada kedua tab."),
        navset_card_tab(
          analysis_tab("Material", "material", "Kategori Material"),
          analysis_tab("Brand / Merek", "brand", "Merek"))))) ,
  nav_panel("Data Explorer",
    layout_sidebar(sidebar = filter_ui("explorer"),
      div(page_title("TRANSACTION DATA", "Data Explorer",
        "Telusuri transaksi pengadaan dan unduh data sesuai filter menu ini."),
        card(card_header("Unduh Dataset"),
          p(class = "muted",
            "Unduhan mengikuti filter Tahun, Area, dan Site. Kotak pencarian tabel hanya memengaruhi tampilan tabel."),
          div(class = "download-actions", downloadButton("download_csv", "Download CSV"),
              downloadButton("download_excel", "Download Excel"))),
        card(card_header("Detail Transaksi PO"), DTOutput("po_table")))))
)

# ------------------------- SERVER ----------------------------
server <- function(input, output, session) {
  overview <- filter_server("overview")
  vendor <- filter_server("vendor")
  material <- filter_server("material")
  explorer <- filter_server("explorer")

  metrics <- function(data) c(
    total_value = sum(data$value, na.rm = TRUE),
    total_po = n_distinct(data$po_number),
    total_vendor = n_distinct(data$supplier[data$supplier != "Tidak tercatat"]),
    total_category = n_distinct(data$material_group[data$material_group != "Tidak tercatat"]))

  # YoY: periode 1 Januari s.d. tanggal terakhir data pada tahun terpilih.
  # Tanggal batas diambil dari seluruh dataset, sehingga Area/Site memakai
  # periode pembanding yang sama; nilai tetap difilter Area/Site terpilih.
  yoy <- reactive({
    chosen <- overview$year()
    if (is.null(chosen) || chosen == ALL) return(list(note = "Pilih satu tahun untuk perbandingan YoY."))
    selected_year <- as.integer(chosen)
    current_dates <- po_data$document_date[!is.na(po_data$year) & po_data$year == selected_year]
    current_dates <- current_dates[!is.na(current_dates)]
    if (!length(current_dates)) return(list(note = "Perbandingan belum tersedia."))
    cutoff <- max(current_dates)
    previous_cutoff <- cutoff %m-% years(1)
    previous_dates <- po_data$document_date[!is.na(po_data$year) & po_data$year == selected_year - 1L]
    previous_dates <- previous_dates[!is.na(previous_dates)]
    if (!length(previous_dates) || max(previous_dates) < previous_cutoff) {
      return(list(note = "Data periode pembanding belum lengkap / tersedia."))
    }
    base <- overview$base()
    current <- filter(base, year == selected_year, document_date <= cutoff)
    previous <- filter(base, year == selected_year - 1L, document_date <= previous_cutoff)
    # Tanpa transaksi pada periode sebelumnya, pembagi nol tidak dihitung.
    list(current = metrics(current), previous = metrics(previous),
      caption = paste0("01/01–", format(cutoff, "%d/%m"), "/", selected_year,
        " vs 01/01–", format(previous_cutoff, "%d/%m"), "/", selected_year - 1L))
  })
  for (key in c("total_value", "total_po", "total_vendor", "total_category")) local({
    metric <- key
    output[[metric]] <- renderText({
      x <- metrics(overview$data())[[metric]]
      if (metric == "total_value") compact_rp(x) else fmt_num(x)
    })
    output[[paste0(metric, "_yoy")]] <- renderUI({
      data <- yoy()
      if (!is.null(data$note)) return(div(class = "yoy yoy-flat", data$note))
      before <- data$previous[[metric]]
      if (is.na(before) || before <= 0) return(div(class = "yoy yoy-flat",
        "Perbandingan belum tersedia", div(class = "yoy-note",
          "Nilai tahun sebelumnya nol atau tidak positif.")))
      change <- (data$current[[metric]] - before) / before * 100
      css <- if (change > 0) "yoy-up" else if (change < 0) "yoy-down" else "yoy-flat"
      symbol <- if (change > 0) "▲" else if (change < 0) "▼" else "—"
      div(class = paste("yoy", css), paste(symbol, paste0(fmt_num(abs(change), 1), "%"),
        if (change > 0) "naik" else if (change < 0) "turun" else "tetap"),
        div(class = "yoy-note", data$caption))
    })
  })

  group_summary <- function(data, column) {
    data %>% group_by(across(all_of(column))) %>%
      summarise(total_value = sum(value, na.rm = TRUE), total_po = n_distinct(po_number),
        total_vendor = n_distinct(supplier[supplier != "Tidak tercatat"]),
        total_site = n_distinct(site[site != "Tidak tercatat"]),
        last_po_date = latest_date(document_date), .groups = "drop") %>%
      arrange(desc(total_value))
  }
  vendor_summary <- reactive({
    vendor$data() %>% group_by(supplier) %>%
      summarise(total_value = sum(value, na.rm = TRUE), total_po = n_distinct(po_number),
        total_material = n_distinct(material_group[material_group != "Tidak tercatat"]),
        total_site = n_distinct(site[site != "Tidak tercatat"]),
        last_po_date = latest_date(document_date), .groups = "drop") %>% arrange(desc(total_value))
  })
  material_summary <- reactive(group_summary(material$data(), "material_group"))
  brand_summary <- reactive(group_summary(material$data(), "brand"))

  insight <- function(data, label, noun, raw = NULL) {
    if (!nrow(data)) return(div(class = "insight", "Tidak ada data untuk kombinasi filter ini."))
    ranked <- data[order(-data$total_value), , drop = FALSE]
    first <- ranked[1, , drop = FALSE]
    total <- sum(data$total_value)
    share <- if (total > 0 && all(data$total_value >= 0)) paste0(" (",
      fmt_num(100 * first$total_value / total, 1), "% dari total)") else ""
    frequent <- data[order(-data$total_po), , drop = FALSE][1, , drop = FALSE]
    div(class = "insight", div(class = "insight-title", "SOROTAN DATA"),
      div(strong(paste0(noun, " dengan nilai terbesar: ")), first[[label]], " — ",
        compact_rp(first$total_value), share, "."),
      div(strong("PO terbanyak: "), frequent[[label]], " — ", fmt_num(frequent$total_po),
        " PO unik."),
      if ("Tidak tercatat" %in% data[[label]]) div(class = "muted",
        "Kategori Tidak tercatat tetap disertakan agar total nilai sesuai dataset."))
  }
  output$overview_insight <- renderUI({
    data <- overview$data()
    content <- insight(group_summary(data, "supplier"), "supplier", "Vendor")
    unknown_value <- sum(is.na(data$value))
    missing_dates <- sum(is.na(data$document_date))
    tagList(content,
      if (unknown_value || missing_dates) div(class = "insight",
        "Kualitas data: ", fmt_num(unknown_value), " baris tanpa nilai PO dan ",
          fmt_num(missing_dates),
        " baris tanpa tanggal valid dalam data terpilih. Nilai kosong diabaikan dalam penjumlahan; tanggal kosong tidak masuk tren atau filter tahun."))
  })
  output$vendor_insight <- renderUI(insight(vendor_summary(), "supplier", "Vendor"))
  output$material_insight <- renderUI(insight(material_summary(), "material_group",
    "Kategori material"))
  output$brand_insight <- renderUI(insight(brand_summary(), "brand", "Merek"))

  output$trend <- renderPlotly({
    data <- overview$data() %>% filter(!is.na(document_date)) %>%
      mutate(month = floor_date(document_date, "month")) %>% group_by(month) %>%
      summarise(value = sum(value, na.rm = TRUE), .groups = "drop") %>% arrange(month)
    check_rows(data)
    months <- data.frame(month = seq(min(data$month), max(data$month), by = "month"))
    data <- left_join(months, data, by = "month")
    data$value[is.na(data$value)] <- 0
    unit <- money_unit(data$value)
    plotted <- data$value / unit$divisor
    tips <- paste0(format(data$month, "%m/%Y"), "<br>", fmt_rp(data$value))
    plot_ly(x = data$month, y = plotted, type = "scatter", mode = "lines+markers", height = 560,
      line = list(color = BLUE, width = 3), marker = list(color = BLUE, size = 7),
      hovertext = tips, hovertemplate = "%{hovertext}<extra></extra>") %>%
      layout(showlegend = FALSE, paper_bgcolor = "white", plot_bgcolor = "white", separators = ",.",
        font = list(family = "Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif", size = 12, color = "#52627A"),
        margin = list(l = 90, r = 35, t = 30, b = 65),
        xaxis = list(title = "Bulan", tickformat = "%m/%Y", nticks = 10, showgrid = FALSE),
        yaxis = list(title = paste0("Nilai PO (Rp", if (nzchar(unit$label)) paste0(" ",
          unit$label) else "", ")"),
          tickformat = ",.2f", rangemode = "tozero", gridcolor = "#EDF1F7",
            zeroline = FALSE)) %>% plot_config()
  })
  output$overview_vendor <- renderPlotly(rank_chart(group_summary(overview$data(), "supplier"),
    "supplier", "total_value", money = TRUE))
  output$vendor_value <- renderPlotly(rank_chart(vendor_summary(), "supplier", "total_value",
    money = TRUE))
  output$vendor_frequency <- renderPlotly(rank_chart(vendor_summary(), "supplier", "total_po"))
  output$vendor_table <- renderDT({
    data <- vendor_summary() %>% rename(Vendor = supplier, `Total Nilai PO` = total_value,
      `Jumlah PO Unik` = total_po, `Kategori Material` = total_material,
      `Jumlah Site` = total_site, `Tanggal PO Terakhir` = last_po_date)
    make_table(data, money = "Total Nilai PO", counts = c("Jumlah PO Unik", "Kategori Material",
      "Jumlah Site"))
  })

  # Pendaftaran output kedua tab secara terpisah.
  for (kind in c("material", "brand")) local({
    prefix <- kind
    summary_data <- if (prefix == "material") material_summary else brand_summary
    column <- if (prefix == "material") "material_group" else "brand"
    title <- if (prefix == "material") "Kategori Material" else "Merek"
    output[[paste0(prefix, "_value")]] <- renderPlotly(rank_chart(summary_data(), column,
      "total_value", money = TRUE))
    output[[paste0(prefix, "_frequency")]] <- renderPlotly(rank_chart(summary_data(), column,
      "total_po"))
    output[[paste0(prefix, "_distribution")]] <- renderPlotly(donut_chart(summary_data(), column))
    output[[paste0(prefix, "_table")]] <- renderDT({
      data <- summary_data()
      names(data) <- c(title, "Total Nilai PO", "Jumlah PO Unik", "Jumlah Vendor", "Jumlah Site",
        "Tanggal PO Terakhir")
      make_table(data, money = "Total Nilai PO", counts = c("Jumlah PO Unik", "Jumlah Vendor",
        "Jumlah Site"))
    })
  })
  output$po_table <- renderDT({
    data <- explorer$data() %>% select(document_date, po_number, area, site, supplier,
      material, material_group, short_text, brand, quantity, value) %>%
      rename(Tanggal = document_date, `Nomor PO` = po_number, Area = area, Site = site,
        Vendor = supplier, `Kode Material` = material, `Kategori Material` = material_group,
        Deskripsi = short_text, Merek = brand, Kuantitas = quantity, `Nilai PO` = value)
    make_table(data, money = "Nilai PO", decimals = "Kuantitas", page_length = 20)
  })
  output$download_csv <- downloadHandler(
    filename = function() paste0("PT_BIMA_Procurement_", Sys.Date(), ".csv"),
    content = function(file) write.csv(explorer$data(), file, row.names = FALSE,
      fileEncoding = "UTF-8"))
  output$download_excel <- downloadHandler(
    filename = function() paste0("PT_BIMA_Procurement_", Sys.Date(), ".xlsx"),
    content = function(file) {
      data <- explorer$data()
      wb <- createWorkbook()
      addWorksheet(wb, "Data PO")
      writeData(wb, "Data PO", data)
      freezePane(wb, "Data PO", firstRow = TRUE)
      addFilter(wb, "Data PO", rows = 1, cols = seq_along(data))
      if (nrow(data)) {
        addStyle(wb, "Data PO", createStyle(numFmt = '"Rp" #,##0.00'),
          rows = 2:(nrow(data) + 1), cols = match("value", names(data)), gridExpand = TRUE)
        addStyle(wb, "Data PO", createStyle(numFmt = "dd/mm/yyyy"),
          rows = 2:(nrow(data) + 1), cols = match("document_date", names(data)), gridExpand = TRUE)
      }
      setColWidths(wb, "Data PO", cols = seq_along(data), widths = 20)
      saveWorkbook(wb, file, overwrite = TRUE)
    })
}

shinyApp(ui = ui, server = server)
